    h result = {};
    dict_set(result, "_type", "Null");
    return result;
}

fn value_type(v) {
    return dict_get(v, "_type");
}

fn value_is_int(v) {
    return dict_get(v, "_type") == "HInt";
}

fn value_is_float(v) {
    return dict_get(v, "_type") == "HFloat";
}

fn value_is_string(v) {
    return dict_get(v, "_type") == "String";
}

fn value_is_bool(v) {
    return dict_get(v, "_type") == "Bool";
}

fn value_is_array(v) {
    return dict_get(v, "_type") == "HArray";
}

fn value_is_null(v) {
    return dict_get(v, "_type") == "Null";
}

fn value_is_singularity(v) {
    if dict_get(v, "_type") == "HInt" {
        return dict_get(v, "is_singularity") == 1;
    }
    return 0;
}

fn value_is_numeric(v) {
    h t = dict_get(v, "_type");
    if t == "HInt" {
        return 1;
    }
    if t == "HFloat" {
        return 1;
    }
    return 0;
}

fn approx_eq(a, b, eps) {
    return abs(a - b) < eps;
}

fn test_phi_constants() {
    assert_true(approx_eq(PHI, 1.618033, 0.001), "PHI approx 1.618");
    assert_true(approx_eq(PHI_INV, 0.618033, 0.001), "PHI_INV approx 0.618");
    assert_true(approx_eq(PHI_SQ, 2.618033, 0.001), "PHI_SQ approx 2.618");
    assert_true(approx_eq(PHI * PHI_INV, 1.0, 1e-6), "PHI * PHI_INV ~ 1");
    print("PASS: test_phi_constants");
}

fn test_fibonacci() {
    assert_eq(fibonacci(0), 0, "fib(0)=0");
    assert_eq(fibonacci(1), 1, "fib(1)=1");
    assert_eq(fibonacci(10), 55, "fib(10)=55");
    assert_eq(fibonacci(11), 89, "fib(11)=89");
    assert_eq(fibonacci(12), 144, "fib(12)=144");
    print("PASS: test_fibonacci");
}

fn test_compute_him() {
    h him_0 = compute_him(0);
    assert_true(approx_eq(him_0, 0.0, 0.001), "him(0) = 0");

    # HIM should be in [0, 0.5]
    h him_42 = compute_him(42);
    assert_true(him_42 >= 0.0, "him(42) >= 0");
    assert_true(him_42 <= 0.5, "him(42) <= 0.5");

    print("PASS: test_compute_him");
}

fn test_hfloat() {
    h f = hfloat_new(3.14);
    assert_eq(dict_get(f, "_type"), "HFloat", "type = HFloat");
    assert_true(approx_eq(dict_get(f, "value"), 3.14, 0.001), "value = 3.14");
    print("PASS: test_hfloat");
}

fn test_value_is_numeric() {
    assert_true(value_is_numeric(value_int(1)), "int is numeric");
    assert_true(value_is_numeric(value_float(1.0)), "float is numeric");
    assert_true(value_is_numeric(value_string("x")) == false, "string not numeric");
    assert_true(value_is_numeric(value_null()) == false, "null not numeric");
    print("PASS: test_value_is_numeric");
}

fn test_hint_resonance() {
    h fib_89 = hint_new(89);
    assert_true(dict_get(fib_89, "resonance") > 0.95, "fib 89 high resonance");

    h nonfi = hint_new(100);
    assert_true(dict_get(nonfi, "resonance") < 0.95, "100 lower resonance");

    print("PASS: test_hint_resonance");
}

fn load_history() {
    h content = file_read(BENCH_DB)
    if content == null { return {} }
    h parsed = json_parse(content)
    if parsed == null { return {} }
    return parsed
}

fn omc_ask(prompt) {
    h sys = "You are an expert OMC programmer. Output only valid OMC code, no markdown, no explanation."
    return llm_call(prompt, MODEL, sys)
}

fn run_code(code) {
    h result = eval_omc(code)
    return {ok: true, value: result}
}

fn improve_once(code, goal) {
    h prompt = str_concat(
        "Improve this OMC code to better achieve: ", goal, "\n\n",
        "Current code:\n", code, "\n\n",
        "Return only the improved code."
    )
    return strip_fences(omc_ask(prompt))
}

fn critique(code, goal) {
    h prompt = str_concat(
        "Analyze this OMC code for: ", goal, "\n\n",
        "Code:\n", code, "\n\n",
        "List exactly 3 specific improvements. Be terse."
    )
    return llm_call(prompt, MODEL, null)
}

fn apply_critique(code, critique_text) {
    h prompt = str_concat(
        "Apply these improvements to the OMC code:\n\n",
        "Code:\n", code, "\n\n",
        "Improvements:\n", critique_text, "\n\n",
        "Return only the improved OMC code."
    )
    return strip_fences(omc_ask(prompt))
}

fn improve_fn(code, goal) {\n",
        "    h prompt = str_concat(\"Improve this OMC code for \", goal, \":\\n\", code)\n",
        "    h improved = llm_call(prompt, null, \"Write only OMC code, no explanation.\")\n",
        "    return improved\n",
        "}

fn bsearch(arr, target) {\n    h lo = 0\n    h hi = arr_len(arr) - 1\n    while lo <= hi {\n        h mid = (lo + hi) / 2\n        if arr[mid] == target { return mid }\n        if arr[mid] < target { lo = mid + 1 }\n        else { hi = mid - 1 }\n    }\n    return -1\n}

fn rev_str(s) {\n    h out = \"\"\n    h i = str_len(s) - 1\n    while i >= 0 {\n        out = str_concat(out, str_slice(s, i, i + 1))\n        i = i - 1\n    }\n    return out\n}

fn _pad_right(s, width) {
    h str = to_str(s)
    h len = str_len(str)
    h out = str
    h i = len
    while i < width {
        out = str_concat(out, " ")
        i = i + 1
    }
    return out
}

fn _pad_left(s, width) {
    h str = to_str(s)
    h len = str_len(str)
    h out = ""
    h i = len
    while i < width {
        out = str_concat(out, " ")
        i = i + 1
    }
    return str_concat(out, str)
}

fn test_closures() {
    fn make_adder(n) {
        return fn(x) {
            return x + n;
        };
    }
    h add5 = make_adder(5);
    assert_eq(add5(10), 15);
    assert_eq(add5(20), 25);
}

fn make_adder(n) {
        return fn(x) {
            return x + n;
        };
    }

fn _expensive(n) {
    _call_count = _call_count + 1
    return n * n
}

fn test_memoize2_computes_correctly() {
    fn _add(a, b) { return a + b }
    h m = cache.memoize2(_add)
    assert_eq(m(3, 4), 7, "memoize2 3+4")
    assert_eq(m(10, 20), 30, "memoize2 10+20")
}

fn test_lru_memoize() {
    h count = 0
    fn _fn(n) { count = count + 1 return n * 3 }
    h m = cache.lru_memoize(_fn, 5)
    assert_eq(m(2), 6, "lru_memoize computes")
    m(2)
    m(2)
    assert_eq(count, 1, "subsequent calls cached")
}

fn _fn(n) { count = count + 1 return n * 3 }

fn test_batch_cached_results() {
    fn _triple(n) { return n * 3 }
    h bc = cache.lru_new(20)
    h out = cache.batch_cached(_triple, [1, 2, 3], bc)
    assert_eq(out[0], 3,  "batch[0]")
    assert_eq(out[1], 6,  "batch[1]")
    assert_eq(out[2], 9,  "batch[2]")
}

fn test_batch_cached_deduplicates() {
    h fn_calls = 0
    fn _inc(n) { fn_calls = fn_calls + 1 return n }
    h bc = cache.lru_new(20)
    cache.batch_cached(_inc, [1, 2, 1, 2, 1], bc)
    assert_eq(fn_calls, 2, "only 2 unique keys computed")
}

fn _inc(n) { fn_calls = fn_calls + 1 return n }

fn test_while_counts() {
    h n = 0;
    while n < 10 { n = n + 1; }
    assert_eq(n, 10, "while counts to 10");
}

fn loop(n) { h i = 0; while i < n { i = i + 1; } return i; }

fn loop(n) { h k = 0; while k < n { k = k + 1; } return k; }

fn bench_lcg_chain(n, seed) {
    h s = seed;
    h i = 0;
    while i < n {
        s = _prom_lcg_step(s);
        i += 1;
    }
    return s;
}

fn run_lcg_bench() {
    h iters = 50000;
    h t0 = time_ms();
    h final_state = bench_lcg_chain(iters, 42);
    h t1 = time_ms();
    h ms = t1 - t0;
    print(concat_many("[LCG]  ", to_string(iters), " calls  final=",
        to_string(final_state), "  time=", to_string(ms), "ms"));
    return ms;
}

fn make_corpus() {
    h text = "abcabcabcabcabcabcabcabcabc";
    h ids = [];
    h i = 0;
    while i < str_len(text) {
        h ch = str_slice(text, i, i + 1);
        h idx = 0;
        if ch == "b" { idx = 1; }
        elif ch == "c" { idx = 2; }
        arr_push(ids, idx);
        i += 1;
    }
    return ids;
}

fn build_small_model(seed) {
    h L1 = prom_linear_new(3, 8, seed);
    h L2 = prom_linear_new(8, 3, dict_get(L1, "rng_state"));
    h m = dict_new();
    dict_set(m, "L1", L1);
    dict_set(m, "L2", L2);
    return m;
}

fn run_train_bench() {
    h steps = 500;
    h ms = train_steps(steps, 42);
    h per_step = ms / steps;
    print(concat_many("[TRAIN] ", to_string(steps), " steps  total=",
        to_string(ms), "ms  per_step=", to_string(per_step), "ms"));
    return ms;
}

fn run_dispatch_overhead(n) {
    h s = 1;
    h t0 = time_ms();
    h i = 0;
    while i < n {
        s = _prom_lcg_step(s);
        i += 1;
    }
    h t1 = time_ms();
    h ms = t1 - t0;
    return ms;
}

fn decide_action(pv, pn, hl, al) {
    h flee = hl * (pn + pv);
    h attack = pn * (1 - hl);
    h chase = pv * (1 - pn) * (1 - flee);
    
    if flee { return 4; }
    if attack { return 3; }
    if chase { return 2; }
    return 1;
}

fn otape_new() {
    h t = dict_new();
    dict_set(t, "vals",  []);
    dict_set(t, "grads", []);
    dict_set(t, "ops",   []);
    dict_set(t, "p1s",   []);
    dict_set(t, "p2s",   []);
    dict_set(t, "n",     0);
    return t;
}

fn _otape_push(tape, val, op, p1, p2) {
    h id = dict_get(tape, "n");
    arr_push(dict_get(tape, "vals"),  val);
    arr_push(dict_get(tape, "grads"), _mat_zeros_like(val));
    arr_push(dict_get(tape, "ops"),   op);
    arr_push(dict_get(tape, "p1s"),   p1);
    arr_push(dict_get(tape, "p2s"),   p2);
    dict_set(tape, "n", id + 1);
    return id;
}

fn otape_const(tape, mat) { return _otape_push(tape, mat, 0, -1, -1); }

fn otape_var(tape, mat)   { return _otape_push(tape, mat, 1, -1, -1); }

fn otape_value(tape, id) { return arr_get(dict_get(tape, "vals"),  id); }

fn otape_grad(tape, id)  { return arr_get(dict_get(tape, "grads"), id); }

fn otape_add(tape, a_id, b_id) {
    h a = arr_get(dict_get(tape, "vals"), a_id);
    h b = arr_get(dict_get(tape, "vals"), b_id);
    return _otape_push(tape, _mat_add(a, b), 2, a_id, b_id);
}

fn otape_sub(tape, a_id, b_id) {
    h a = arr_get(dict_get(tape, "vals"), a_id);
    h b = arr_get(dict_get(tape, "vals"), b_id);
    return _otape_push(tape, _mat_sub(a, b), 6, a_id, b_id);
}

fn otape_matmul(tape, a_id, b_id) {
    h a = arr_get(dict_get(tape, "vals"), a_id);
    h b = arr_get(dict_get(tape, "vals"), b_id);
    return _otape_push(tape, _mat_mul(a, b), 3, a_id, b_id);
}

fn otape_relu(tape, a_id) {
    h a = arr_get(dict_get(tape, "vals"), a_id);
    return _otape_push(tape, _mat_relu(a), 4, a_id, -1);
}

fn otape_mse(tape, pred_id, target_id) {
    h pred   = arr_get(dict_get(tape, "vals"), pred_id);
    h target = arr_get(dict_get(tape, "vals"), target_id);
    h loss   = _mat_mse_scalar(pred, target);
    return _otape_push(tape, [[loss]], 5, pred_id, target_id);
}

fn _mat_set_elem(mat, i, j, v) {
    # Set mat[i][j] = v in-place (exploits Rc-shared row references).
    h row = arr_get(mat, i);
    row[j] = v;
}

fn _mat_add_into(dst, src) {
    # dst[i][j] += src[i][j] in-place.
    h rows = arr_len(dst);
    h i = 0;
    while i < rows {
        h dr = arr_get(dst, i);
        h sr = arr_get(src, i);
        h j = 0;
        while j < arr_len(dr) {
            dr[j] = arr_get(dr, j) + arr_get(sr, j);
            j += 1;
        }
        i += 1;
    }
}

fn _mat_zeros_like(m) {
    h out = [];
    h i = 0;
    while i < arr_len(m) {
        h src = arr_get(m, i);
        h row = [];
        h j = 0;
        while j < arr_len(src) { arr_push(row, 0.0); j += 1; }
        arr_push(out, row);
        i += 1;
    }
    return out;
}

fn _mat_zeros(rows, cols) {
    h out = [];
    h i = 0;
    while i < rows {
        h row = [];
        h j = 0;
        while j < cols { arr_push(row, 0.0); j += 1; }
        arr_push(out, row);
        i += 1;
    }
    return out;
}

fn _mat_add(a, b) {
    h out = [];
    h i = 0;
    while i < arr_len(a) {
        h ra = arr_get(a, i);
        h rb = arr_get(b, i);
        h row = [];
        h j = 0;
        while j < arr_len(ra) { arr_push(row, arr_get(ra, j) + arr_get(rb, j)); j += 1; }
        arr_push(out, row);
        i += 1;
    }
    return out;
}

fn _mat_sub(a, b) {
    h out = [];
    h i = 0;
    while i < arr_len(a) {
        h ra = arr_get(a, i);
        h rb = arr_get(b, i);
        h row = [];
        h j = 0;
        while j < arr_len(ra) { arr_push(row, arr_get(ra, j) - arr_get(rb, j)); j += 1; }
        arr_push(out, row);
        i += 1;
    }
    return out;
}

fn _mat_transpose(a) {
    h rows = arr_len(a);
    h cols = arr_len(arr_get(a, 0));
    h out = [];
    h j = 0;
    while j < cols {
        h row = [];
        h i = 0;
        while i < rows { arr_push(row, arr_get(arr_get(a, i), j)); i += 1; }
        arr_push(out, row);
        j += 1;
    }
    return out;
}

fn _mat_scale(a, s) {
    h out = [];
    h i = 0;
    while i < arr_len(a) {
        h ra = arr_get(a, i);
        h row = [];
        h j = 0;
        while j < arr_len(ra) { arr_push(row, arr_get(ra, j) * s); j += 1; }
        arr_push(out, row);
        i += 1;
    }
    return out;
}

fn _tape_lcg(s) {
    h s2 = s * 1103515245 + 12345;
    return s2 - (s2 / 2147483648) * 2147483648;
}

fn _tape_uniform(s, bound) {
    h s2 = _tape_lcg(s);
    h f = (s2 - (s2 / 1000000) * 1000000) / 500000.0 - 1.0;
    if f < 0.0 { f = 0.0 - f; }
    h v = f * bound * 2.0 - bound;
    h r = dict_new();
    dict_set(r, "v", v);
    dict_set(r, "s", s2);
    return r;
}

fn otape_zeros_matrix(rows, cols) { return _mat_zeros(rows, cols); }

fn my_func(a, b, c) { return a + b + c }

fn other_fn(x) { return x * 2 }

fn _sgd_update(mat, grad, lr) {
    h rows = arr_len(mat);
    h r = 0;
    while r < rows {
        h mr = arr_get(mat, r);
        h gr = arr_get(grad, r);
        h j = 0;
        while j < arr_len(mr) {
            mr[j] = arr_get(mr, j) - lr * arr_get(gr, j);
            j += 1;
        }
        r += 1;
    }
}

fn test_logic() {
    h x = true and false;
    if x { return 1; }
    return 0;
}

fn hbit_q(v, scale) {
    let qmax = 127;
    let a = v * scale;
    if a > qmax { a = qmax; }
    if a < -qmax { a = -qmax; }
    return a;
}

fn hbit_dq(q_val, scale) {
    return q_val * scale;
}

fn sigmoid_phi(x) {
    let clamped = x;
    if clamped > sigmoid_bound { clamped = sigmoid_bound; }
    if clamped < -sigmoid_bound { clamped = -sigmoid_bound; }
    let folded = phi.fold(clamped, 2);
    return 1.0 * pow(2.71828, -folded) + 1.0;
}

fn phi_attn_score(q, k, depth, w_depth) {
    let raw = q * k * 0.044715; // 1/sqrt(depth) precomputed for depth=512
    let depth_signal = sin(raw * phi * depth) * w_depth;
    let score = raw + depth_signal;
    return phi.fold(score, 2);
}

fn phi_ffn_act(x, layer_idx) {
    let phi_phase = (layer_idx * 1.618034) - floor(layer_idx * 1.618034);
    let gate = 0.5 + 0.5 * sin(x * 1.618034 + phi_phase * 6.2831853);
    return x * gate;
}

fn gelu_approx(x) {
    let cdf = 0.5 * (1.0 + tanh(sqrt(2.0 * 0.318309886) * (x + 0.044715 * x * x * x)));
    return x * cdf;
}

fn simulate_layer(layer_idx) {
    let attn_score = evo_attn_layer(layer_idx, 32);
    let ffn_score = evo_ffn_layer(layer_idx, 24);
    let fused = phi.fold(attn_score + ffn_score, 3);
    let q_fused = hbit_q(fused, 0.01);
    let dq_fused = hbit_dq(q_fused, 0.01);
    return dq_fused;
}

fn test_fn(x) {
    let a = 1.0 / (1.0 + x);
    return a;
}

fn sigmoid_phi(x) {
    let clamped = x;
    let folded = phi.fold(clamped, 2);
    return 1.0 / (1.0 + folded);
}

    print("test");
}

    let a = x + y;
    let b = z / 1000.0;
    let c = phi.fold(a + b, 2);
    return c;
}

    return 1.0 / 2.0;
}

fn test2() {
    return 1.0 / (1.0 + 2.0);
}

fn test3() {
    return 1.0 / sqrt(4.0);
}

fn test4(x) {
    return phi.fold(x, 2);
}

fn test5(x) {
    let folded = phi.fold(x, 2);
    return 1.0 / (1.0 + pow(2.71828, -folded));
}

fn binary_search(arr, target) {
    h lo = 0;
    h hi = arr_len(arr) - 1;
    while lo <= hi {
        h mid = (lo + hi) / 2;
        h v = arr_get(arr, mid);
        if v == target {
            return mid;
        }
        if v < target {
            lo = mid + 1;
        } else {
            hi = mid - 1;
        }
    }
    return -1;
}

fn merge(left, right) {
    h n_left = arr_len(left);
    h n_right = arr_len(right);
    h result = arr_new(n_left + n_right, 0);
    h i = 0;
    h j = 0;
    h k = 0;
    while i < n_left && j < n_right {
        if arr_get(left, i) <= arr_get(right, j) {
            arr_set(result, k, arr_get(left, i));
            i = i + 1;
        } else {
            arr_set(result, k, arr_get(right, j));
            j = j + 1;
        }
        k = k + 1;
    }
    while i < n_left {
        arr_set(result, k, arr_get(left, i));
        i = i + 1;
        k = k + 1;
    }
    while j < n_right {
        arr_set(result, k, arr_get(right, j));
        j = j + 1;
        k = k + 1;
    }
    return result;
}

fn merge_sort(arr) {
    h n = arr_len(arr);
    if n <= 1 {
        return arr;
    }
    h mid = n / 2;
    h left = arr_new(mid, 0);
    h right = arr_new(n - mid, 0);
    h i = 0;
    while i < mid {
        arr_set(left, i, arr_get(arr, i));
        i = i + 1;
    }
    h j = 0;
    while j < n - mid {
        arr_set(right, j, arr_get(arr, mid + j));
        j = j + 1;
    }
    return merge(merge_sort(left), merge_sort(right));
}

fn bubble_sort(arr) {
    h n = arr_len(arr);
    h i = 0;
    while i < n {
        h j = 0;
        while j < n - i - 1 {
            if arr_get(arr, j) > arr_get(arr, j + 1) {
                h tmp = arr_get(arr, j);
                arr_set(arr, j, arr_get(arr, j + 1));
                arr_set(arr, j + 1, tmp);
            }
            j = j + 1;
        }
        i = i + 1;
    }
    return arr;
}

fn insertion_sort(arr) {
    h n = arr_len(arr);
    h i = 1;
    while i < n {
        h key = arr_get(arr, i);
        h j = i - 1;
        while j >= 0 && arr_get(arr, j) > key {
            arr_set(arr, j + 1, arr_get(arr, j));
            j = j - 1;
        }
        arr_set(arr, j + 1, key);
        i = i + 1;
    }
    return arr;
}

fn partition(arr, lo, hi) {
    h pivot = arr_get(arr, hi);
    h i = lo - 1;
    h j = lo;
    while j < hi {
        if arr_get(arr, j) <= pivot {
            i = i + 1;
            h tmp = arr_get(arr, i);
            arr_set(arr, i, arr_get(arr, j));
            arr_set(arr, j, tmp);
        }
        j = j + 1;
    }
    h tmp2 = arr_get(arr, i + 1);
    arr_set(arr, i + 1, arr_get(arr, hi));
    arr_set(arr, hi, tmp2);
    return i + 1;
}

fn quick_sort_inner(arr, lo, hi) {
    if lo < hi {
        h p = partition(arr, lo, hi);
        quick_sort_inner(arr, lo, p - 1);
        quick_sort_inner(arr, p + 1, hi);
    }
    return arr;
}

fn quick_sort(arr) {
    return quick_sort_inner(arr, 0, arr_len(arr) - 1);
}

fn max_of(a, b) { if a > b { return a; } return b; }

fn min_of(a, b) { if a < b { return a; } return b; }

fn abs_val(x) { if x < 0 { return -x; } return x; }

fn arr_sum(arr) {
    h acc = 0;
    h i = 0;
    while i < arr_len(arr) {
        acc = acc + arr_get(arr, i);
        i = i + 1;
    }
    return acc;
}

fn arr_product(arr) {
    h acc = 1;
    h i = 0;
    while i < arr_len(arr) {
        acc = acc * arr_get(arr, i);
        i = i + 1;
    }
    return acc;
}

fn arr_reverse(arr) {
    h n = arr_len(arr);
    h result = arr_new(n, 0);
    h i = 0;
    while i < n {
        arr_set(result, i, arr_get(arr, n - 1 - i));
        i = i + 1;
    }
    return result;
}

fn is_prime(n) {
    if n < 2 { return 0; }
    h i = 2;
    while i * i <= n {
        if n % i == 0 { return 0; }
        i = i + 1;
    }
    return 1;
}

fn gcd(a, b) {
    while b != 0 {
        h tmp = b;
        b = a % b;
        a = tmp;
    }
    return a;
}

fn lcm(a, b) {
    return a / gcd(a, b) * b;
}

fn int_pow(base, exp) {
    h result = 1;
    while exp > 0 {
        if exp % 2 == 1 {
            result = result * base;
        }
        base = base * base;
        exp = exp / 2;
    }
    return result;
}

fn arr_find_max(arr) {
    h best = arr_get(arr, 0);
    h i = 1;
    while i < arr_len(arr) {
        if arr_get(arr, i) > best {
            best = arr_get(arr, i);
        }
        i = i + 1;
    }
    return best;
}

fn arr_find_min(arr) {
    h best = arr_get(arr, 0);
    h i = 1;
    while i < arr_len(arr) {
        if arr_get(arr, i) < best {
            best = arr_get(arr, i);
        }
        i = i + 1;
    }
    return best;
}

fn arr_contains(arr, val) {
    h i = 0;
    while i < arr_len(arr) {
        if arr_get(arr, i) == val { return 1; }
        i = i + 1;
    }
    return 0;
}

fn sigmoid(x) {
    return 1.0 / (1.0 + exp(-x));
}

fn relu(x) {
    if x > 0.0 { return x; }
    return 0.0;
}

fn dot(a, b) {
    h acc = 0.0;
    h i = 0;
    while i < arr_len(a) {
        acc = acc + arr_get(a, i) * arr_get(b, i);
        i = i + 1;
    }
    return acc;
}

fn matmul(A, B, rows_a, cols_a, cols_b) {
    h C = arr_new(rows_a * cols_b, 0.0);
    h i = 0;
    while i < rows_a {
        h j = 0;
        while j < cols_b {
            h s = 0.0;
            h k = 0;
            while k < cols_a {
                s = s + arr_get(A, i * cols_a + k) * arr_get(B, k * cols_b + j);
                k = k + 1;
            }
            arr_set(C, i * cols_b + j, s);
            j = j + 1;
        }
        i = i + 1;
    }
    return C;
}

fn arr_mean(arr) {
    h s = 0.0;
    h n = arr_len(arr);
    h i = 0;
    while i < n {
        s = s + arr_get(arr, i);
        i = i + 1;
    }
    return s / to_float(n);
}

fn arr_variance(arr) {
    h m = arr_mean(arr);
    h acc = 0.0;
    h n = arr_len(arr);
    h i = 0;
    while i < n {
        h d = arr_get(arr, i) - m;
        acc = acc + d * d;
        i = i + 1;
    }
    return acc / to_float(n);
}

fn clamp(x, lo, hi) {
    if x < lo { return lo; }
    if x > hi { return hi; }
    return x;
}

fn lerp(a, b, t) {
    return a + (b - a) * t;
}

fn str_repeat(s, n) {
    h result = "";
    h i = 0;
    while i < n {
        result = result + s;
        i = i + 1;
    }
    return result;
}

fn swap_arr(arr, i, j) {
    h tmp = arr_get(arr, i);
    arr_set(arr, i, arr_get(arr, j));
    arr_set(arr, j, tmp);
}

fn arr_range(start, end) {
    h n = end - start;
    h result = arr_new(n, 0);
    h i = 0;
    while i < n {
        arr_set(result, i, start + i);
        i = i + 1;
    }
    return result;
}
